## Embedded Files
53206d02aa9be4df11b78d11f3fc5fdc6f1e0166: $$f:\mathbb{R}^n \to \mathbb{R}^m$$

733b487e61b49e1942cfed43919e6110c480f923: $$J: \mathbb{R}^n \to \mathbb{R}^{m\times n}$$

f1b210ecf8fa032ca463a23961c4ea84dd65052a: $$J(x)=\begin{bmatrix}\dfrac{\partial f_1}{\partial x_1}(x)&\cdots&\dfrac{\partial f_1}{\partial x_n}(x)\\ \vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\ \dfrac{\partial f_m}{\partial x_1}(x)&\cdots&\dfrac{\partial f_m}{\partial x_n}(x)\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}(\nabla f_1(x))^\top\\ \vdots\\ (\nabla f_m(x))^\top\end{bmatrix}$$

6a5a187a9c17bc9b2ecdc3d32fd8ea406ccc5301: $$f(x+h)=f(x)+J(x).h+o(||h||^2)$$

28213bf32772f6ab73d81adc4119101b13a4a3d9: $$df_x: \mathbb{R}^n \to \mathbb{R}^m$$

123a822b61aa277263a91785971f7ea96119ce2b: $$\nabla_xf(g(x))=J_{f}(x)^T \nabla_fg(f(x))$$

3db22a3cb3df9e1b843e91517610be40064c482e: $$g:\mathbb{R}^m \to \mathbb{R}^k$$

e1894b967800b5e632527cd768c79c59c2aa8092: $$J_{g \circ f}(x)=J_g(f(x)).J_f(x)$$

07f4afdcb91b44eca1af15a35dff1ca4619bef1c: $$g(f(x))$$

e85e522dc88775af7ed6b5f9c64145bd035bb3c7: $$J_g(f(x))=
\begin{bmatrix}
\dfrac{\partial g_1}{\partial f_1(x)}(f(x))&\cdots&\dfrac{\partial g_1}{\partial f_m(x)}(f(x))\\ \vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\ \dfrac{\partial g_k}{\partial f_1(x)}(f(x))&\cdots&\dfrac{\partial g_k}{\partial f_m(x)}(f(x))
\end{bmatrix}=
\begin{bmatrix}(\nabla_f g_1(f(x)))^\top\\ \vdots\\ (\nabla_f g_m(f(x)))^\top\end{bmatrix}$$

b236d35bf73d58c243288cb0e138f95c494f8112: $$\frac{\partial g}{\partial x_j}=\sum_{i=1}^m \frac{\partial g}{\partial f_i}\,\frac{\partial f_i}{\partial x_j},\qquad j=1,\dots,n$$

2d466ca5de609afa3bbd0f41b632c64296164552: $$\frac{dz}{dt}=\frac{\partial z}{\partial x} \frac{dx}{dt}+ \frac{\partial z}{\partial y} \frac{dy}{dt}$$
